## Corpus Check
- 1133 files · ~931,974 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 17030 nodes · 26396 edges · 1800 communities (1310 shown, 490 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 284 edges (avg confidence: 0.65)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e9109df8`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
